                                                                                        50. Duty to enforce tobacco-fr tt provisions
                                                                                            I/ It shall be the duty of the owner or another
                                                                                               appropriate person in charge of the
                                                                                               management of the public place or public
                                                                                               conveyance for which tobacco smoking, use
                                                                                               or sale is prohibited to ensure that no one
                                                                                               smoke, use, or sale any tobacco product. and
                                                                                               to ban the placement of an ashtray or other
                                                                                               comparable devices intended for tobacco use
                                                                                               in such plac'i.

                                                                              credential, and introduce his identity and
